# 实款亮点与FAQ · 当前文案审核

由当前逐ID改稿生成，审稿原件为各商品目录的copy.json；本页不另行维护事实。相同真实产品且正文完全相同的链接合并展示，色码或版本答案不同则分别展示。159条未闭环历史链接不在本页。

亮点解释具体结构与选款价值；产品问题解释本款，采购流程问题仅在适用时复用。提交、平台审核、正式字段和公开呈现分开计数，实时状态见[当前执行](当前执行.json)。五条离线重复链接与受保护源链接只供本地文案审阅，不能据此认为重新发布或已修改。

## BQ-ABH219 · BQ-ABH219

对应链接：[1601969936536](商品/1601969936536/copy.json)

**产品亮点**

Lace-up ankle boots with a side zipper and a defined lugged rubber sole. The laces set the front fit, while the inner-side zipper provides a separate opening for putting the boot on. A PU upper and Black or Brown color direction give this style a classic casual-boot appearance.

**FAQ**

**Which color and EU size choices are listed?**

Brown: EU 35–40; Black: EU 35–40.

**How do the laces and zipper work together?**

The front laces adjust the upper, and the inner-side zipper opens the boot. Set the laces for your fit, then use the zipper when putting it on or taking it off.

**What should I assess on a sample?**

Adjust the laces with the intended socks, then check toe room, instep fit and heel hold before choosing the bulk EU size mix.

**What is the minimum order for the original style?**

The listed original-style minimum is 2 pairs. Quantity pricing is shown on this page; logo and custom packaging proposals are quoted separately.

**How can I request a sample?**

Send the selected color or lining option, EU size and delivery postcode for a sample quotation covering the pair, courier charge and proposed dispatch date.

**What should I send for logo or packaging requirements?**

Send the logo artwork, preferred placement, packaging brief and order quantity. The proposal for this style will set out the applicable minimum, cost and sample process.

**How can I get a delivered quotation?**

Send pair quantities by color and EU size, the destination postcode or port and the packaging request. The quotation separates product cost and freight and includes the proposed dispatch and transit schedule.

Send your logo artwork, preferred placement, packaging brief and order quantity. We can review the brief for this style and provide a proposal with the applicable minimum, cost and sample process.

**How can I get a delivered quotation?**

Send the color-size quantities and destination postcode or port, plus any packaging request. The quotation separates product cost from freight and sets out the dispatch plan and estimated transit time for your chosen shipping method.
